- **gbx-bench-all**: Run the full local benchmark pipeline **sequentially**: generate the corpus, run the heavyweight (Scala/JNI, Docker) then the lightweight (pyrx, venv) benchmarks on the *same* corpus, then compare them. Heavy and light run one-after-the-other (never concurrently) so they don't contend for CPU and skew each other's timings. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-all.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-changed**: Benchmark **only** the functions affected by the current changes, then write authoritative per-function store records from that run. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-changed.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-clean**: Prune `test-logs/bench/` — ad-hoc run directories, orphaned store records, or everything. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-clean.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-cluster**: Submit the heavy-vs-light benchmark as a one-off notebook job to a **provisioned** Databricks cluster. Both APIs run on the same cluster (the true same-hardware comparison); results append to the `bench_results` Delta table and `comparison.csv`/`summary.md` land on the configured Volume. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-cluster.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-compare**: Join a run's heavyweight + lightweight JSONL shards into a heavy-vs-light **speedup + output-consistency** report (`comparison.csv` + `summary.md`), in the isolated pyrx venv. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-compare.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-gen-data**: Generate the seeded benchmark corpus (GeoTIFF tiles + `corpus.json` manifest) in the isolated pyrx venv, then run the validity gate. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-gen-data.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-generate-vector-corpus**: Generate the scaled vector benchmark corpus (polygon seed → per-format transcoding → ×N replicas) using the pyrx venv. Runs locally for small-scale validation; use the bench cluster for the full 1M×100 corpus. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-generate-vector-corpus.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-heavyweight**: Run the heavyweight (Scala/JNI RasterX) benchmark in the `geobrix-dev` container over a generated corpus, writing a `heavyweight.jsonl` shard (same schema as the lightweight runner). Pure-core opens each tile with GDAL and calls `RST_*.execute`; spark-path times the registered `gbx_rst_*` Column on a local Spark DataFrame. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-heavyweight.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-lightweight**: Run the lightweight (pyrx) benchmark runner in the isolated venv, over a generated corpus, in pure-core and/or spark-path mode. Writes a JSONL result shard (with output fingerprints for consistency comparison on pure-core rows). ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-lightweight.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-readers**: Time the light raster reader (`raster_gbx`) per-file in pure-local mode, or over a corpus directory via the Spark data source in spark-path mode. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-readers.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-seed**: Bootstrap (or rebuild) the authoritative per-function benchmark store from a full run. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-seed.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-stalecheck**: Advisory pre-push warning for changed functions whose benchmark record is missing or stale. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-stalecheck.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-bench-status**: Print a coverage/parity/performance scorecard from the authoritative benchmark store. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-bench-status.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-ci-act**: Validate `.github/workflows/*.yml` changes before pushing by running them locally via [act](https://github.com/nektos/act). Catches ~80% of CI bugs (typos, missing env, action SHA pin issues, step ordering, composite-action structure errors) without a push-and-iterate loop. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-ci-act.md) · ★24)

- **gbx-ci-setup**: Installs and configures the GitHub CLI (`gh`) for CI management (trigger workflows, watch runs, fetch logs). - **gbx-ci-status**: Shows recent GitHub Actions workflow runs for the current branch (build main, doc tests, etc.). ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-ci-status.md) · ★24)

- **gbx-coverage-gaps**: **Analyze coverage data and identify improvement opportunities** ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-coverage-gaps.md) · ★24)

- **gbx-data-generate-minimal-bundle**: Generates a minimal sample-data bundle under `sample-data/Volumes/main/default/test-data/geobrix-examples/` by extracting subsets from the full bundle using bounding boxes around NYC (default: center of Manhattan) and London (default: center of London). Vector data is limited by a configurable row count; raster data is clipped to the bbox (all pixels within bounds). Used so doc tests can pass in C ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-data-generate-minimal-bundle.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-data-generate-vector-corpus**: Generates N synthetic vector features in a chosen `*_gbx` light-writer format via the light vector writers, for scaled benchmarking. - **gbx-data-push-jar**: Runs **mvn clean package -DskipTests** and uploads **both** jars it produces (one build): the product **target/*-jar-with-dependencies.jar** → **GBX_ARTIFACT_VOLUME**/ (the init script loads it onto the cluster), and the bench **target/*-tests.jar** → the **bundle volroot** (`/Volumes/$GBX_BUNDLE_VOLUME_CATALOG/$GBX_BUNDLE_VOLUME_SCHEMA/$GBX_BUNDLE_VOLUME_NAME/`, where the bench launcher attaches ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-data-push-jar.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-data-push-wheel**: Builds the JAR first (unless **GBX_BUNDLE_SKIP_JAR_UPLOAD=1**), then runs **python3 -m build** and uploads the wheel to **GBX_ARTIFACT_VOLUME**/ (no subpath). Overwrites if file already exists. Set **GBX_BUNDLE_SKIP_WHEEL_UPLOAD=1** to skip wheel build/upload. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-data-push-wheel.md) · ★24)

- **gbx-lint-scalastyle**: Runs ScalaStyle on `src/main/scala` using the same config as CI (`scalastyle-config.xml`). Use this to catch style warnings and errors before pushing. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-lint-scalastyle.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-mcp-auth**: Show which configured MCP servers are authenticated versus which need auth, at a glance. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-mcp-auth.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-perf-vectorscan**: Scan the pyrx core (`python/geobrix/src/databricks/labs/gbx/pyrx/core/*.py`) for **vectorizable anti-patterns** — per-pixel / per-element work done in Python instead of NumPy/SciPy. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-perf-vectorscan.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-qc-fix**: Idempotently re-apply GeoBrix's local adjustments to the user-global QC judge (`~/.claude/qc-judge/`). Databricks force-updates those files periodically and can wipe the adjustments; re-run this after any suspected update. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-qc-fix.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-review-round**: Run [Isaac Review](https://github.com/databricks-eng) on a **scoped slice** of the branch instead of the full branch-vs-main diff. On a release branch (e.g. `beta/0.5.0` → `main`) that full diff is ~1000 files — an expensive, low-signal review. This command defaults to reviewing only the commits you're about to push and refuses an oversized scope unless you opt in, so the full-branch pass never fi ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-review-round.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-security-codeql**: Runs **CodeQL** on the repo using the CodeQL CLI. No GitHub license required: the CLI is free to use locally; only uploading to GitHub Code Scanning requires Code Security to be enabled for the repo. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-security-codeql.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-bindings**: Verify every registered GeoBrix function exists across all language bindings. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-bindings.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-bundle-databricks**: **Default:** Pushes the runner notebook to the workspace (`GBX_BUNDLE_RUNNER_NOTEBOOK_PATH`) and runs it **on the cluster** (`CLUSTER_ID`) as a one-off job. The bundle executes on Databricks, so Volume paths work without running anything on your machine. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-bundle-databricks.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-docs**: Runs **all** documentation tests by invoking **gbx-test-python-docs**, **gbx-test-sql-docs**, and **gbx-test-scala-docs** in sequence. Pass-through options (e.g. `--skip-build`, `--no-sample-data-root`) are forwarded so `GBX_SAMPLE_DATA_ROOT` and build behavior are consistent. Use for pre-commit or CI-style validation. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-docs.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-docs-examples**: Structural guards for the RasterX documentation example tabs and output tables. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-docs-examples.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-function-info**: Re-inventories `function-info.json` (with placeholders for full coverage) and runs the function-info test suite inside the **geobrix-dev** Docker container. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-function-info.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-notebooks**: Runs notebooks **cell-by-cell** (no Jupyter kernel) by default: discovers `notebooks/tests/fixtures/*.ipynb` and `notebooks/sample-data/*.ipynb`, executes each code cell with `exec()`, and reports per-cell status. **All runs happen inside the `geobrix-dev` Docker container** via `docker exec`. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-notebooks.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-notebooks-serverless**: Imports local `.ipynb` notebooks into the Databricks workspace (stripping `%pip`/`%restart_python` cells — they fail in Serverless JOB compute), then submits each as a one-time Serverless notebook job via `jobs.submit`, polling to terminal and stopping on the first failure. Dependencies are injected via the Serverless environment spec (not `%pip`). ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-notebooks-serverless.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-parity**: Run the light-vs-heavy (`pyvx` vs `vectorx`) parity suites as a **real gate**: rebuild the assembly JAR, stage it where the light tier loads it, run the integration-marked parity suites, and fail if nothing actually ran. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-parity.md) · ★24)
- **gbx-test-primitive-databricks**: Pushes the **primitive runner** notebook to the workspace and runs it **on the cluster** as a one-off job. The notebook tests only fundamental Volume operations via the SDK: (a) volume exists, (b) create subdirs, (c) read from volume, (d) write to volume, (e) write to local then copy to volume. Use this to confirm the cluster can see and use Unity Catalog volumes before running the full bundle. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-primitive-databricks.md) · ★24)

- **gbx-test-python**: Runs Python unit tests (non-documentation tests) using pytest. ([databrickslabs/geobrix](https://github.com/databrickslabs/geobrix/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/gbx-test-python.md) · ★24)
